The Faulcon DeLacy Sidewinder mod's files: where each came from. Written by copy_sidewinder (src/sidewinder/convert/).
Elite Dangerous is Frontier Developments'; every file below taken from it is theirs (install version 2026.10.23.334925).
Grades are the plan's (docs/spec-mods-sidewinder.md): A = Elite's own bytes, E = recreated.
Elite's original assets come from the decoded export (re/elite/frea_export/sidewinder/decoded, made from the install by the export tool, out of git): its README gives the exact vertex decode and the spaces.
Models: LOD0 only: Elite's LOD switch rule is not known.

Not shipped: the pg_sidewinder_extendedengine drive bolt-ons (the export's engine_left / engine_right: rigs PG_SideWinder_ExtendedEngineLeft / Right, models driveboltonleft, driveboltonright, plateleft, plateright, fin, material SideWinder_DriveBoltOn) are the Proving Ground arena's by the evidence at rest: their pg_ names, as the arena's pg_sidewinder hull and cockpit, and the item database listing them only beside the CQC loadouts.

Chair: 3 of the motion graph's 41 states and 13 of the 91 chair clips are shipped. Left out: the crew roles' Action_* modes (fighter and gunner seats, their idles and transitions), the buggy's modes, and chair_activatefast, which no state plays: a solo pilot's chair never reaches them (re/elite/notes/agent_motiongraph.md 2.4, 2.6).

Sounds: the open events left out because the banks at rest lack their media: event_3b16927e (laser_fire: which of the unnamed events is the shot (and its LOD rule) is in the hardpoint's data, not at rest): its media is not at rest; event_8acf4d18 (laser_fire: which of the unnamed events is the shot (and its LOD rule) is in the hardpoint's data, not at rest): its media is not at rest; event_a266f96c (laser_fire: which of the unnamed events is the shot (and its LOD rule) is in the hardpoint's data, not at rest): its media is not at rest; event_a7e81522 (laser_fire: which of the unnamed events is the shot (and its LOD rule) is in the hardpoint's data, not at rest): its media is not at rest.

Ship UI sounds: values of the cockpit interfaces' tables and HUD events of the game with no event in any bank (Elite posts their ids and nothing answers: silence), not shipped: AlarmIncomingMissle_b, CloseBack_b_Cockpit, CloseCancel_b_Cockpit, Complete_ModulePowerUp_CockpitInternalPanel, MessageSurfaceDiscovery, ModulePowerUp_b_CockpitInternalPanel, ModulePowerUp_e_CockpitInternalPanel, ModulePowerUp_Pulse_CockpitInternalPanel, ModuleRepair_CockpitCanopy_CockpitInternalPanel, OpenBack_b_Cockpit, OpenForward_b_Cockpit, PPRank_0_Powerplay_3d, PPRank_1_Powerplay_3d, PPRank_2_Powerplay_3d, PPRank_3_Powerplay_3d, PPRank_4_Powerplay_3d, Select_ModulePowerUp_CockpitInternalPanel, TelepresenceHide, ValueUpLocked_b__Cockpit, WarningViolation_b.

The ship UI: not shipped, as no export holds them whole: the textures fsshud.pdiffuse_alphatexture (material fsshud: in no export), mc_roledesignation_background.pdiffuse_alphatexture (material mc_roledesignation_background: the UI export's copy is damaged: tail size 49664, expected 17920), saahud.pdiffuse_alphatexture (material saahud: in no export), uibackground.pcloudynesstexture (material fsshud: in no export), uibackground.pcloudynesstexture (material saahud: in no export), uiedgedetection_frontend.pscanlinestexture (material effectplane: in no export), uifrontend_parallax_smoke2.pdiffuse_alphatexture (material notifications: in no export), uifrontend_parallax_stripe.pcloudynesstexture (material notifications: in no export). Not shipped either: the UIModuleManager's per-panel scales and tuning block (what they multiply was not found, E), the multicrew seats' alternative chairs, and the movies out of A10's scope.

The ship UI's scanner and holograms (ui/hud3d/): not shipped, as the mod has nothing they would show: the scanner's contact shapes, stalks, overlays and spheres; the target hologram's station, belt, signal and point-of-interest models.

binds/ControlPad.binds (A): A copy of Elite's key file.
  from: Elite install ControlSchemes/ControlPad.binds

Values are the banks'.
